select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select es.id, eg.game_name as name, es.amount as price from es_setup as es inner join es_game as eg on eg.id=es.game_id where es.is_current = 'Y'
select ei.id, CONCAT('Token: ' , ei.token_count, ', Balance Qty: ', (ei.qty - get_used_item_qty(ei.id)) , ' , Item: ',  ei.name) AS n from es_item as ei WHERE ei.qty > 0 and ei.token_count > 0  order by ei.token_count, ei.name

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '1b6f9785-1f11-44d5-9462-c56f0896dc27-1765464854' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{65D9FD08}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1151 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='1b6f9785-1f11-44d5-9462-c56f0896dc27-1765464854' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='1b6f9785-1f11-44d5-9462-c56f0896dc27-1765464854' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '1b6f9785-1f11-44d5-9462-c56f0896dc27-1765464854' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;
select ifnull(max(created_at_unix), 0) as uts from es_billing where rfid='{398B42B8}' and guid='8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289'
select * from es_customer where rfid='{398B42B8}' and guid='8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' and is_new=1
select bonus_amount from es_bonus where amount=50 order by amount desc
select amount, bonus_amount from es_bonus order by amount
select id, name, time, price from es_package order by name, price
select es.id, eg.game_name as name, es.amount as price from es_setup as es inner join es_game as eg on eg.id=es.game_id where es.is_current = 'Y'
select ei.id, CONCAT('Token: ' , ei.token_count, ', Balance Qty: ', (ei.qty - get_used_item_qty(ei.id)) , ' , Item: ',  ei.name) AS n from es_item as ei WHERE ei.qty > 0 and ei.token_count > 0  order by ei.token_count, ei.name

SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='8dec1eda-d66b-465b-a784-566b34b9b994-1765048289' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '37f53555-3c5a-4ad1-b2cb-54827a9a0fa7-1765469758' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{65D9DF2A}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1153 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='37f53555-3c5a-4ad1-b2cb-54827a9a0fa7-1765469758' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='37f53555-3c5a-4ad1-b2cb-54827a9a0fa7-1765469758' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '37f53555-3c5a-4ad1-b2cb-54827a9a0fa7-1765469758' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398CB04D}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398CB04D}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398CB04D}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398CB04D}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398CB04D}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=575 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'aeadf62b-9782-43ee-b3a7-c1afe905297e-1761420939' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='168ed578-a972-4d25-a5ff-f1ba774fe540-1761769080' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc

select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='056c6359-f84e-4da2-ace8-6927703d3dba-1765141062' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '056c6359-f84e-4da2-ace8-6927703d3dba-1765141062' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{39E2BC2F}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1129 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='056c6359-f84e-4da2-ace8-6927703d3dba-1765141062' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='056c6359-f84e-4da2-ace8-6927703d3dba-1765141062' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '056c6359-f84e-4da2-ace8-6927703d3dba-1765141062' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

SELECT CASE WHEN ec.created_at = ep.created_at THEN 'NEW' ELSE 'OLD' END AS card_type, COUNT(*) AS payment_count, SUM(ep.recharge_amount) AS total_amount FROM es_payment AS ep INNER JOIN es_customer AS ec ON ec.guid = ep.guid AND ec.rfid = ep.rfid WHERE ec.is_vip = 1 and ep.created_at>='2025-12-11 00:00:00' and ep.created_at<='2025-12-11 23:59:59'  GROUP BY CASE WHEN ec.created_at = ep.created_at THEN 'NEW' ELSE 'OLD' END

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.created_at>='2025-12-11 00:00:01' and _ep.created_at<='2025-12-11 23:59:59'  and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
